The `public contributions` by users in Afghanistan on `2026/11/23 5:30 AM UTC`. This list contains users from `Afghanistan` and cities `Kabul` `Kandahar` `Herat` `Kunduz` `Lashkargah` `Ghazni` `Khost` `Zaranj`.

There are `138 countries` and `674 cities` can be found [here](https://github.com/gayanvoice/top-github-users).

There are `948 users`  in Afghanistan. You need at least `3 followers` to be on this list.

🏅 Looking for a shareable ranking badge? Visit the [Afghanistan GitHub user ranking page](https://github.com/gayanvoice/top-github-users-ranking/blob/main/markdown/afghanistan.md) to view the country rankings and copy the ready-made badge snippet for your GitHub profile or README.
